Front/back gradient via FFT:
```
def lse_grad_a(a, p, n):
    M_fft = fft_len(2*n-1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M_fft)
    W = np.fft.rfft(p, M_fft)  # p length n
    Wr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M_fft)
    back = np.fft.irfft(A * W, M_fft)[:n]
    fwd = np.fft.irfft(A * Wr, M_fft)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return back + fwd
```
Need verify `p[::-1]` length n; if p[0] nonzero, reversed includes p[0] at end. Good.

Memory: A size M_fft/2+1 ~8193 for 16384; W same. Fine.

Can optimize if p uniform? Not needed.

L-BFGS objective function top-level:
```
def lbfgs_objective(x, beta, mode, anchor):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    S = float(x.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return 1e10, np.zeros_like(x)
    a = x / S
    c = self_conv_unique(a)
    if mode == 0: idx slice 0:n
    else: p0...
    cmax = ...
    z = beta*(csel-cmax)
    z = z - z.max()
    ez = np.exp(z)
    p_sel = ez/ez.sum()
    p = np.zeros(n); p[sel]=p_sel
    L = cmax + np.log(ez.sum())/beta
    score = 2.0*n*L + anchor*(S-1.0)**2
    # gradient wrt a
    back/fwd...
    grad_a = 2.0*n * (back+fwd) - 4.0*n*L
    dot = float(np.dot(grad_a, a))  # a sum 1
    grad_x = (grad_a - dot) / S + 2*anchor*(S-1)
    # optionally zero gradient where x<=0? bounds handle; don't
    return score, grad_x
```

Implement stable LSE:
```
def lse_phi(vals, beta):
    cmax = float(vals.max())
    z = beta*(vals-cmax)
    z = z - z.max()
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = ez.sum()
    lse = cmax + (z.max()+np.log(s))/beta  # z.max after shift 0? Actually if shift: LSE = cmax + original_max_shift/beta + log(s)/beta. original max shift = z_pre_shift.max(). We can store.
```
Simpler:
```
z0 = beta*(vals - cmax)
zmax0 = z0.max()
ez = np.exp(z0 - zmax0)
s=ez.sum()
lse = cmax + (zmax0 + np.log(s))/beta
```
Weights = ez/s.

If beta small (10), okay. If beta high 10000, z up to 0, exp stable.

def proxy_score(a, mode):
    n=len(a); S=sum; if S<1e-12 inf; c=self_conv_unique(a); sel = c if full else c[1:]; if len=0 return inf; return 2*n*max(sel)/S^2
```

def init_panels(n):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    y=np.arange(n)/(n-1)
    panels=[]
    panels.append(np.ones(n))
    panels.append(0.5-0.5*np.cos(2πy)) # Hann endpoints 0
    panels.append(0.54-0.46*np.cos(2πy)) # Hamming
    panels.append(0.42-0.5*cos(2πy)+0.08*cos(4πy)) # Blackman
    panels.append(0.35875 -0.48829 cos(2πy)+0.14128 cos(4πy)-0.01168 cos(6πy)) # BH
    panels.append(0.5+0.5*np.cos(2πy)) # raised cosine endpoints? at endpoints 1, center 0? Actually cos gives high edges.
    panels.append(np.exp(-8*(y-0.5)**2))
    panels.append(np.exp(-4*(y-0.5)**2))
    panels.append(np.maximum(0,1-np.abs(2*y-1))) # triangular
    panels.append(1.0 - y**4)
    panels.append((1-y**2)**2)
    # random smooth: generate spectral random, make positive
    rng smooth = 0.5+0.5*np.cos(2π y*np.random?) plus random phases
```

```
def call_evaluator(seq):
    try:
        v = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try: v=float(v)
    except: return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(v): return np.inf
    return v
```

       # print probe

    elif full_rel < off_rel: mode=0 else 1
    return mode

Run L-BFGS:
```
def run_lbfgs(a, beta, mode, maxiter=2000, maxfun=10000, anchor=1e-9, deadline=None):
    n=len(a)
    x=normalize(a).copy()
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for n # If beta low maybe values upper 1 enough
    opts={'ftol':1e-15,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':40}
    try:
       res=minimize(lbfgs_objective, x, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta, mode, anchor), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
       x=np.asarray(res.x, float)
    except Exception as e:
       print('lbfgs fail', e); return x
    x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    S=x.sum()
    if S<1e-9: return x
    return x/S
```

def lbfgs_objective(x, beta, mode, anchor):
   ...
   n=len(x); M=2*n-1; M_fft=...
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, M_fft)
   c_full=np.fft.irfft(A*A, M_fft)[:n]
   sel_vals = c_full if mode==0 else c_full[1:]
   ... p
   W=np.fft.rfft(p, M_fft); Wr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M_fft)
   back=np.fft.irfft(A*W, M_fft)[:n]
   fwd=np.fft.irfft(A*Wr, M_fft)[n-1:2*n-1]
   grad_L=back+fwd
```

Let's detail functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _fft_power(length):
    n=1
    while n < length: n <<=1
    return n
```
But loops for many calls overhead; use bit_length:
```
def fft_len_for(full_len):
    return 1 << (max(1, full_len-1).bit_length())
```
For length 1, 1.

`self_conv_unique(a)`:
```
def self_conv_unique(a):
    n=len(a); M=2*n-1; Mf=fft_len_for(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, Mf)[:n]
```
`proxy_score(a, mode)`:
```
def proxy_score(a, mode):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=len(a); S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=self_conv_unique(a)
    vals=c if mode==0 else c[1:]
    if vals.size==0: return np.inf
    M=float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```
But if mode off and n=1 vals empty.

`grad_sel_conv(a,p)`: returns sum p_k c_k derivative for selected p (length n with p sel):
```
def grad_selected(a,p):
    n=len(a); M=2*n-1; Mf=fft_len_for(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, Mf)
    Pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], Mf)
    back=np.fft.irfft(A*P, Mf)[:n]
    fwd=np.fft.irfft(A*Pr, Mf)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return back+fwd
```

"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def fft_len_for(full_len):
    if full_len <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (full_len - 1).bit_length()

def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-12:
       return np.ones_like(a)/float(len(a))
    return a/S

def run_lbfgs_schedule(a, mode, deadline, label):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    best_eval=np.inf; best_eval_a=a.copy()
    work=a.copy(); work_proxy=proxy_score(a,mode)
    current=a.copy()
    schedules = ((10,300,1500),(30,400,2000),(100,600,3000),(300,900,4500),(1000,1200,6000))
    for beta,maxit,maxfun in schedules:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30: break

       new = normalize_nonneg(new)

          work_proxy=pr; work=new.copy()

`lp_alternating` top-level:
```
def lp_fista(a, mode, M, max_iters, beta, x_init, deadline, check_every=500):
   n=len(a)
   if x_init is not None: x=normalize? no, x not normalized. If x_init sum maybe; use clip and if sum<0 x=ones/n.
   else x=np.ones(n)/n
   u=x.copy(); tv=1.0
   M=2n-1; Mf=...
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
   sum_a2=np.dot(a,a)
   L = beta*(4.0*float(n)*sum_a2 + 0.1) + 1e-12  # maybe too small? beta=500, sum_a2~1/n~1e-4, 4n sum~4, L~2000. step 5e-4. If x~n? Fine.
   step=1.0/L
   sel_len = n if mode0 else n-1
   sel = slice(0,n) or (1,n)
   linear_coeff=1.0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      B=np.fft.rfft(x, Mf)
      cfull=np.fft.irfft(A*B, Mf)[:2*n-1]
      vals = cfull if mode0 else cfull[1:]
      z=beta*(vals-M)
      zmax=z.max(); ez=np.exp(z-zmax); s=ez.sum(); p_sel=ez/s
      p=np.zeros(n); p[1:]=p_sel if mode off else p[:]
      P=np.fft.rfft(p, Mf)
      Bt=np.fft.irfft(A*P, Mf)[:n]
      grad = Bt - linear_coeff
      v=u-step*grad
      x_new=np.maximum(v,0.0)
      tnew=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(1+4*tv*tv))
      u=x_new + (tv-1)/tnew*(x_new-x)
      u=np.maximum(u,0)
      x=x_new; tv=tnew
   return x
```

`peak_search`:
```
def peak_search(a, mode, deadline, max_steps=200, rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a; best_pr=proxy_score
    for step in range(max_steps):
       if time>deadline: break
       c=self_conv_unique(a)
       vals=c if mode0 else c[1:]
       M=vals.max()
       # top fraction
       m=len(vals)
       ktop=max(1, int(round(max(5, 0.001*m))))
       thresh = np.sort(vals)[-ktop] maybe expensive; use np.partition(vals, -ktop)[-ktop]
       active = np.where(vals >= thresh - 1e-15*max(1,M))[0]
       p_sel = np.ones(len(active))/len(active)
       p=zeros(n); if mode0: p[active]=p_sel else p[active+1]=p_sel
       g=grad_selected(a,p) # derivative of average active c wrt a
       # project zero mean
       g = g - float(np.mean(g))
       maxg=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       scale=(a.sum()/n)*0.05/maxg if maxg>0 else 0.1
       base=min(10.0, scale)
       improved=False
       for mult in [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
          d=base*mult
          at=normalize_nonneg(a - d*g)
          pr=proxy_score(at,mode)
          if pr<best_pr-1e-12:
             best_pr=pr; best=at; a=at; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          # small smooth perturbation
          noise=smooth_noise(n,rng,2.0)
          at=normalize_nonneg(a*(1+0.02*noise))
          pr=proxy_score(at,mode)
          if pr<best_pr: a=at; best_pr=pr; best=at
          else:
             # if many fails, perturb bigger? break
             if step%20==0: a=normalize_nonneg(a*(1+0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)-0.025?)) maybe
    return best
```
But `g - mean(g)` changes sum direction; If `g` is derivative of average c, reducing `c` average by moving a against g. Normalize handles sum. Good.

`smooth_noise` top-level:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, decay=3.0):
    nfft=1 << (n-1).bit_length()
    f=np.fft.rfftfreq(nfft)*nfft
    amp=np.exp(-decay*np.arange(len(f))/max(len(f),1))
    re=rng.standard_normal(len(f))*amp
    im=rng.standard_normal(len(f))*amp
    spec=np.zeros(nfft, complex)
    spec[:len(f)]=re+1j*im
    # Hermitian
    v=np.fft.ifft(spec).real[:n]
    v-=v.mean()
    nv=np.linalg.norm(v)
    if nv>0: v/=nv
    return v
```
Could be called many times; okay.

       for sign,mult in [(1,0.3),(1,1.0),(-1,1.0),(1,3.0)]:

           if pr < best_pr -1e-9:
              ev=call_evaluator(at.tolist())
              if not finite or ev < best_ev: best_ev=ev; best=at; best_pr=pr
       # multiplicative noise
       noise=smooth_noise(n,rng,3.0)
       for s in [0.05,0.15,0.35]:
          at=normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(a*(1+s*noise),0))
          pr=...
    return best,best_ev
```

def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(120,budget_s)-30
    rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    # get parent
    parent=None

       if 'height_sequence_1' in globals(): p=globals()['height_sequence_1']; ...
    except: pass

    parent_eval=None

       parent_eval=call_evaluator(parent.tolist())
       if parent_eval finite and parent_eval < 2.0 and min(abs(parent_eval-proxy_score(parent,1)),? ) maybe set mode=1

    main = best_nonconstant or first nonconstant
    if time left > 600: best_off = run_lbfgs_schedule(main, 1, deadline_off, 'mainOFF')
    # 2) if parent different from main and time left: run off schedule on parent shorter

def get_parent():
    try:
       p = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
       return None
    arr=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size <=0: return None
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    S=arr.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return None
    return arr/S

However c(a) for a normalized sum1; if x changes, a changes with sum fixed? But is the gradient of L with respect to a on the simplex (not all a) sufficient? L(a) as a function on all a, and then normalized. The chain rule formula gives gradient of f wrt x. Yes.

```
def run_lbfgs_schedule(a, mode, deadline, label):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_eval = call_evaluator(a.tolist())
    best_proxy = proxy_score(a, mode)
    work_a = a.copy()
    work_proxy = best_proxy
    current = a.copy()
    beta_schedule = ((10,300,1500),(30,500,2500),(100,800,4000),(300,1000,5000),(1000,1500,7000))
    for beta,maxit,maxfun in beta_schedule:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60: break
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
       # adjust maxfun based on remaining? crude: max(200, int(remaining/0.02)*0.1?) too high. We'll set fixed, but if remaining<120 skip.
       if remaining < 60: break
       new = run_lbfgs(current, beta, mode, maxit, maxfun, 1e-9)
       if new is None: continue
       new = normalize_nonneg(new)
       pr = proxy_score(new, mode)
       ev = call_evaluator(new.tolist())
       if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_eval - 1e-12:
           best_eval = ev; best_a = new.copy()
       else:
           # if eval not better, maybe still update best if no eval?
           if best_eval == np.inf and pr < best_proxy:
               best_eval = np.inf; best_a = new.copy(); best_proxy=pr
       if pr < work_proxy - 1e-11:
           work_proxy=pr; work_a=new.copy()
       # current: use new if proxy improvement or eval improvement, else maybe work
       if pr < work_proxy or np.isfinite(ev) and ev <= best_eval:
           current = new.copy()
       else:
           # if no improvement, use work to avoid drift
           current = work_a if work_proxy < proxy_score(current, mode) else current
       print label beta score ...
    # one very high beta from best
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
       start = best_a if np.isfinite(best_eval) else work_a
       new = run_lbfgs(start, 5000, mode, 2000, 8000, 1e-10)
       new=normalize_nonneg(new); pr=... ev=...
       update
    # return best by eval if finite else work; work
    if np.isfinite(best_eval): return best_a, best_eval, work_a
    return work_a, np.inf, work_a
```

Now, implement `make_initial_candidates` top-level. Need ensure if parent length not n, resample. For windows, use np.arange(n)/(n-1); for n=1? n large.
```
def make_initial_candidates(n, rng, parent):
    cands=[]
    if parent is not None and np.isfinite(parent).all():
        if len(parent)==n: cands.append(parent.copy())
        else: cands.append(resample_sequence(parent, n))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    cands.append(np.ones(n))
    cands.append(0.5-0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
    cands.append(0.54-0.46*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
    cands.append(0.42-0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)+0.08*np.cos(4*np.pi*x))
    cands.append(0.35875 -0.48829*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)+0.14128*np.cos(4*np.pi*x)-0.01168*np.cos(6*np.pi*x))
    for w in [2,4,6,8]: cands.append(np.exp(-w*(x-0.5)**2))
    cands.append(np.maximum(0.0,1.0-np.abs(2.0*x-1.0)))
    cands.append(0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
    cands.append(0.5+0.25*np.cos(4*np.pi*x))
    for k in range(6):
        spec=np.fft.rfft? To generate random smooth:
        m=n//2+1
        f=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)*n
        amp=1.0/(1.0+0.05*np.arange(m))
        re=rng.standard_normal(m)*amp
        im=rng.standard_normal(m)*amp
        v=np.fft.irfft(re+1j*im, n).real
        v=np.abs(v)+0.2*np.exp(-4*(x-0.5)**2) + 1e-3
        cands.append(v)
    for k in range(3):
        v=np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))+1e-3
        # light smoothing via uniform convolution maybe use np.convolve small kernel [1,2,1]
        v=np.convolve(v, np.array([0.25,0.5,0.25]), mode='same')
        cands.append(v)
    # clean
    out=[]
    for v in cands:
       v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
       if len(v)!=n: v=resample_sequence(v,n)
       v=np.clip(v,0,None)+1e-7
       S=v.sum()
       if S<1e-12: continue
       v=v/S
       out.append(v)
    return out
```
Using `np.convolve` for small n=7000 with 3 kernel direct? It uses direct maybe okay. Could avoid to save. Use uniform_filter? scipy.ndimage not necessary. Just add.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    m=len(a)
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    xo=np.linspace(0,1,m); xnew=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    return np.interp(xnew, xo, a)
```

`choose_probe_mode`: Need small n 96/128 to avoid direct conv cost. Use normalized arrays. For each, compute pf,po. If call evaluator inf, skip. Choose mode with lower relative error. But if evaluator exact, full rel 0. If off hidden, off rel 0. Good.
```
def choose_probe_mode():
    n=128
    rng=np.random.default_rng(11)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    seqs=[np.ones(n), 0.5-0.5*cos(2πx), np.exp(-5*(x-0.5)**2)+0.1, np.abs(rng.normal(n))+1]
    full_rel=off_rel=0.0; count=0
    for s in seqs:
        s=normalize_nonneg(s)
        ev=call_evaluator(s.tolist())
        if not finite: continue
        pf=proxy_score(s,0); po=proxy_score(s,1)
        full_rel += abs(ev-pf)/max(1.0,abs(ev))
        off_rel += abs(ev-po)/max(1.0,abs(ev))
        count +=1
    if count==0: return 1
    if full_rel <= off_rel: return 0
    return 1
```
If exact full, full_rel=0. If hidden off, off_rel=0. If both similar maybe choose off by default? If full_rel<=off_rel with equal 0? exact full chooses full. Good. If no evaluator, off default to explore. But without eval, final no scores; okay.

`select_search_modes(probe_mode, parent_eval)`:
```
def select_search_modes(probe_mode, parent_eval):
    modes=[]

Implement top-level `param_objective(coeffs, base, bases, scale)`:
```
def param_objective(coeffs, base, bases, scale):
    a=base.copy()
    for i in range(len(coeffs)):
       c=float(coeffs[i])
       if c != 0.0:
          a = a + scale*c*bases[i]
    if a.size == 0: return np.inf
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
       return np.inf
    a=a/S
    return call_evaluator(a.tolist())
```
This calls evaluator each parameter eval. For Powell, function returns scalar. If `evaluate_sequence` direct costs 0.1s, 500 evals=50s. Good. `maxfev` controls. `maxiter` maybe 100. def build_bases(n, rng, count):
    bases=[]
    freqs=1..10
    for f in range(1, min(12,count//2+?) ):
       b=np.cos(2π f/x) - mean; normalize; bases.append
       b=np.sin(...)-mean
    # random spectral
    while len<bases<count:
       m=n//2+1
       spec_amp = 1/(1+0.1*arange)
       re=rng.normal(len)*spec_amp; im=rng.normal(len)*spec_amp
       v=ifft(re+1j*im).real
       v-=v.mean(); if norm>0 v/=norm; bases.append
    # local bumps: choose random centers, gaussian width random
    while...
       center=rng.random(n); width=rng.uniform(n/20,n/5); b=exp(-0.5*((x-center)/width)^2)-mean; norm.
    return np.stack(bases, axis=1) shape (n,K)

def param_search(base, deadline, max_evals, K=20, scale=0.25):
    n=len(base); rng=np.random.default_rng(123+hash?) # no closure; deterministic
    bases=build_bases(n, rng, K)
    x0=np.zeros(K)
    # initial eval
    best_val = call_evaluator(base.tolist())
    best_x = x0.copy()
    # Run Powell a few times with different scales and initial perturbations
    attempts = [(scale, x0), (0.1, x0), (0.5, rng.normal(K)*0.1)]
    for scale, x0 in attempts:
       if time>deadline: break
       maxfev = min(max_evals, max(50, int((deadline-time)/ (0.2))))? Can't estimate. Use fixed 200 per attempt.
       try:
          res = minimize(param_objective, x0, method='Powell', args=(base, bases, scale), options={'maxfev': 200, 'maxiter':100, 'ftol':1e-12, 'xtol':1e-8})
          xopt=res.x
          # eval xopt
          val=param_objective(xopt, base, bases, scale)
          if val<best_val: best_val=val; best_x=xopt
       except: pass
    # reconstruct best sequence
    a=base.copy(); for i c: a+=scale_best? Need know scale per xopt; xopt corresponds to scale. We must reconstruct with same scale. Store best_scale.

Let's draft functions in final code mentally.

`fft_len_for`:
```
def fft_len_for(full_len):
    if full_len <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (max(1, full_len - 1).bit_length())
```
For full_len=2n-1, okay.

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
       return np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
       a=np.ones_like(a)
       S=float(a.sum())
    return a / S
```

`get_parent_sequence`:
```
def get_parent_sequence():
    g=globals()
    if 'height_sequence_1' not in g: return None
    p=g['height_sequence_1']
    try:
       a=np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       return None
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return None
    return a/S
```

`self_conv_unique`:
```
def self_conv_unique(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=len(a)
    M=2*n-1
    Mf=fft_len_for(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, Mf)[:n]
```

`proxy_score`:
```
def proxy_score(a, mode):
    a=np.asarray(a, float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    S=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=self_conv_unique(a)
    vals=c if mode==0 else c[1:]
    if vals.size==0: return np.inf
    m=float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*m/(S*S))
```

`call_evaluator`:
```
def call_evaluator(seq):
    try:
        v=evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except NameError:
        return np.inf
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        v=float(v)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(v):
        return np.inf
    return v
```
Need if `evaluate_sequence` not defined, NameError. Good.

`select_search_modes`:
```
def select_search_modes(probe_mode, parent_eval):
    modes=[]
    if parent_eval is not None and np.isfinite(parent_eval) and parent_eval < 1.995:
        if probe_mode == 0:
            modes.append(0); modes.append(1)
        else:
            modes.append(1); modes.append(0)
    else:
        modes.append(1)
        if probe_mode == 0:
            # exact full likely; still search off a little? add 1 only.
            pass
    # remove duplicates preserving order
    out=[]
    for m in modes:
        if m not in out: out.append(m)
    return out
```
This always includes off. If parent lower and full indicated, full first. If no parent lower, off only. If probe off but parent lower, off first. Good.

`grad_selected` and `lbfgs_objective`.

`lbfgs_objective` details:
```
def lbfgs_objective(x, beta, mode, anchor):
    x=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=x.size
    if n == 0: return 1e10, np.zeros(0)
    x=np.maximum(np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0), 0.0)
    S=float(x.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    a=x/S
    M=2*n-1
    Mf=fft_len_for(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, Mf)[:n]
    if mode == 0:
       vals=c
       sel_slice = slice(0,n)
    else:
       vals=c[1:]
       sel_slice = slice(1,n)
    if vals.size == 0:
       return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    cmax=float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return 1e10, zeros
    z0=beta*(vals - cmax)
    zmax=float(np.max(z0))
    ez=np.exp(z0 - zmax)
    s=float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0: p_sel=np.ones_like(vals)/vals.size else p_sel=ez/s
    L=cmax + (zmax + np.log(s))/beta
    value=float(2.0*n*L) + float(anchor)*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
    p=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if mode == 0: p[:]=p_sel? But p_sel length n.
    else: p[1:]=p_sel
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, Mf)
    Pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], Mf)
    back=np.fft.irfft(A*P, Mf)[:n]
    fwd=np.fft.irfft(A*Pr, Mf)[n-1:2*n-1]
    grad_L=back+fwd
    grad_b=2.0*n*grad_L
    dot=float(np.dot(grad_b, a))
    grad_x=(grad_b - dot)/S
    grad_x = grad_x + 2.0*anchor*(S-1.0)
    # if not finite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
        grad_x=np.zeros(n)
    return value, grad_x
```
Potential issue: `p[:]=p_sel` if p_sel length n; okay. `p[::-1]` for mode off with p[0]=0, reversed ok.

`run_lbfgs`:
```
def run_lbfgs(a, beta, mode, maxiter, maxfun, anchor=1e-9):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=len(a)
    x=a.copy()
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] * n
    opts={'maxiter':maxiter, 'maxfun':maxfun, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':40}
    try:
       res=minimize(lbfgs_objective, x, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta, mode, anchor), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
       x=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception as e:
       print('L-BFGS failed:', e)
       x=a.copy()
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    S=float(x.sum())
    if S <1e-9: return a
    return x/S
```
`np.clip` upper1000; after normalize maybe.

`lp_fista`: do we need mode in csel? Use `sel_start = 0 if mode==0 else 1`.
```
def lp_fista(a, mode, M, max_iters, beta, x_init, deadline, linear_coeff=1.0):
   n=len(a)
   if x_init is not None:
       x=np.asarray(x_init,float).ravel()
       if x.size!=n: x=resample_sequence(x,n)
       x=np.maximum(np.where(finite,x,0.0),0.0)
       if x.sum()<1e-12: x=np.ones(n)/n
   else: x=np.ones(n)/n
   u=x.copy(); tv=1.0
   Mf=fft_len_for(2*n-1)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
   sum_a2=float(np.dot(a,a))
   L=beta*(4.0*n*sum_a2 + 1.0) + 1e-12
   step=1.0/L
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      B=np.fft.rfft(x, Mf)
      cfull=np.fft.irfft(A*B, Mf)[:2*n-1]
      vals=cfull[sel_start:]
      if vals.size==0: break
      z=beta*(vals - M)
      zmax=float(np.max(z)); ez=np.exp(z-zmax); s=ez.sum(); p_sel=ez/s
      p=np.zeros(n); p[sel_start:]=p_sel
      P=np.fft.rfft(p,Mf)
      Bt=np.fft.irfft(A*P,Mf)[:n]
      grad=Bt - linear_coeff
      v=u - step*grad
      x_new=np.maximum(v,0.0)
      tnew=0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*tv*tv))
      u=x_new + ((tv-1.0)/tnew)*(x_new - x)
      u=np.maximum(u,0.0)
      x=x_new; tv=tnew
   return x
```
If M=0 and vals all 0, z=0, p uniform, grad = Bt -1. Works. If x grows large, Bt large, grad maybe positive. Good.

`lp_alternating` as above. In line search, `ts = [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0]`. Use proxy score. `a_work` for line search update? If best_t gives worse than current, maybe not. We'll compute `best_t` by starting with current a. At the end, if best_t_pr > proxy current, break.
```
def lp_alternating(a, mode, deadline, max_outers=4, fista_iters=3000, beta=400.0):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a; best_pr=proxy_score(a,mode); x_warm=None; no=0
   for outer in range(max_outers):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      c=self_conv_unique(a)
      vals = c if mode==0 else c[1:]
      M=float(np.max(vals))
      x=lp_fista(a, mode, M, fista_iters, beta, x_warm, deadline)
      x_warm=x.copy()
      Sx=float(x.sum())
      if Sx<1e-12 or not finite: break
      xn=x/Sx
      best_t=0.0; best_t_pr=best_pr
      for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0]:
          at=normalize_nonneg((1.0-t)*a + t*xn)
          pr=proxy_score(at,mode)
          if pr < best_t_pr - 1e-12:
             best_t_pr=pr; best_t=t
      if best_t <=0: 
          no +=1
          if no>=2: break
          continue
      at=normalize_nonng((1-best_t)*a + best_t*xn); pr=best_t_pr
      a=at; if pr<best_pr: update no=0 else no++
      if no>=3: break
   return best
```
If no improvement from LP, break.

`peak_search`:

def peak_search(a, mode, deadline, rng=None, max_steps=500):
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(2)
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a; best_pr=proxy_score(a,mode)
   n=len(a)
   for step in range(max_steps):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      c=self_conv_unique(a)
      vals=c if mode==0 else c[1:]
      if vals.size==0: break
      M=float(np.max(vals))
      m=len(vals)
      ktop=max(1, int(round(max(3, 0.001*m))))
      if ktop >= m: thresh=0.0
      else: thresh=float(np.partition(vals, -ktop)[-ktop])
      active=np.where(vals >= thresh - 1e-12*max(1.0, M))[0]
      if active.size==0: active=np.where(vals == M)[0]
      p_sel=np.ones(active.size)/active.size
      p=np.zeros(n)
      if mode==0: p[active]=p_sel
      else: p[active+1]=p_sel
      g=grad_selected(a,p)
      g = g - float(np.mean(g))
      maxg=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
      if not finite or maxg<1e-18:
          noise=smooth_noise(n,rng,3.0); at=normalize_nonneg(a*(1+0.01*noise)); ... continue
      scale = min(10.0, (a.sum()/n)*0.05 / maxg)
      improved=False
      for mult in [0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0]:
          d=scale*mult
          at=normalize_nonneg(a - d*g)
          pr=proxy_score(at,mode)
          if pr < best_pr - 1e-12:
              best_pr=pr; best=at; a=at; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
          # If many no improve, perturb randomly after every 5, break after 30?
          if step % 5 == 0:
              noise=smooth_noise(n,rng,3.0)
              at=normalize_nonng(np.maximum(a*(1.0+0.02*noise),0.0))
              pr=proxy_score(at,mode)
              if pr < best_pr:
                  best_pr=pr; best=at; a=at
          if step > 80 and not improved:
              # maybe break if no progress 30 steps
              break
   return best
```
Need count no improvement to break. Add `stall=0`. If improved stall=0 else++; if stall>40 break.

`random_hill` similar but uses proxy and eval on improvement.
```
def random_hill(a, rng, mode, deadline, max_tries=300):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a; best_pr=proxy_score(a,mode); best_ev=call_evaluator(a.tolist()); stall=0
   n=len(a)
   for i in range(max_tries):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
      d=smooth_noise(n, rng, 3.0)
      mults=[0.01,0.03,0.08,0.15]
      for sign in [1,-1]:
         for mult in mults:
            at=normalize_nonneg(a + sign*mult*d)  # d norm1, mult0.03 -> comp 3e-4? d norm1 comp 0.012, mult .03 ->3e-4 good
            pr=proxy_score(at,mode)
            if pr < best_pr - 1e-10:
               ev=call_evaluator(at.tolist())
               if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_ev:
                   best_ev=ev; best=at; best_pr=pr; stall=0
               elif ev==np.inf:
                   best_pr=pr; best=at; stall=0
               else:
                   # even if eval worse but proxy better? keep best_proxy but not best eval? maybe use at? For final eval, if hidden mismatch, maybe. Keep.
                   best_pr=pr; best=at; stall=0
               break
         if improved: break
      # multiplicative
      for s in [0.05,0.1,0.2]:
         at=normalize_nonng(np.maximum(a*(1.0+s*noise),0))  # need noise, generate once
      if not improved: stall count
      if stall>50: break
   return best,best_ev

`param_search` as above. Need top-level `param_objective` and `build_bases`.

`build_bases` may use n, rng, count. Use `rng` passed.
```
def build_bases(n, rng, count):
    if count <=0: return np.zeros((n,0))
    bases=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for f in range(1, min(9, count//2+3)):
       v=np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x); v=v-v.mean(); bases.append(normalize_basis(v))
       if len(bases)>=count: break
       v=np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x); v=v-v.mean(); bases.append(normalize_basis(v))
    while len(bases)<count:
       m=n//2+1
       f=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)*n
       amp=1.0/(1.0+0.1*f if f? use np.array)
       spec=rng.standard_normal(m)+1j*rng.standard_normal(m)
       # scale by amp and zero mean low? set spec[0]=0 for zero mean
       amp=1.0/(1.0+0.2*np.arange(m))
       spec*=amp; spec[0]=0
       v=np.fft.irfft(spec,n).real
       v=v-v.mean(); bases.append(normalize_basis(v))
    return np.stack(bases,axis=1)
```
`normalize_basis(v)`:
```
def normalize_basis(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
    v=v - v.mean()
    nv=float(np.linalg.norm(v))
    if not finite or nv<1e-12: return np.zeros_like(v)
    return v/nv
```

`param_objective`: Need pass base, bases, scale. Coeffs maybe `res.x`. Use loop over `range(len(coeffs))`. Concern: building a from base each call O(n*K) if not vectorized. K=20, n=7000, 140k multiply; 300 calls=42M, okay. Use if c !=0.
```
def param_objective(coeffs, base, bases, scale):
    coeffs=np.asarray(coeffs,float).ravel()
    if coeffs.size == 0:
       return call_evaluator(base.tolist())
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(coeffs)): return np.inf
    a=base.copy()
    for i in range(coeffs.size):
       c=coeffs[i]
       if c != 0.0:
           a += scale * c * bases[:, i]
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return np.inf
    a=a/S
    return call_evaluator(a.tolist())
```
But divisor: This uses `bases[:,i]` for all n. Need bases shape (n,K). If K=0, bases shape (n,0). Good.

`param_search`:
```
def param_search(base, deadline, max_evals=300, K=18, scale=0.25):
    n=len(base)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(987)
    bases=build_bases(n, rng, K)
    best_val=call_evaluator(base.tolist())
    best_a=base.copy()
    best_coeffs=np.zeros(K)
    best_scale=scale
    initial_points=[np.zeros(K), rng.standard_normal(K)*0.05, rng.standard_normal(K)*0.15]
    for idx, x0 in enumerate(initial_points):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30: break
       opts={'maxfev': max_evals, 'maxiter': 100, 'ftol':1e-12, 'xtol':1e-8}
       try:
          res=minimize(param_objective, x0, method='Powell', args=(base, bases, scale), options=opts)
          xopt=res.x
          val=param_objective(xopt, base, bases, scale)
          if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val - 1e-12:
              best_val=val; best_coeffs=xopt.copy(); best_scale=scale
       except Exception as e:
          print('param search failed', e)
    # reconstruct best
    a=base.copy()
    for i in range(best_coeffs.size):
        if best_coeffs[i] !=0: a += best_scale*best_coeffs[i]*bases[:,i]
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0); S=a.sum(); if S>0 a=a/S
    return a, best_val
```
Powell `maxfev` may not be recognized in some versions? It is. If not, use `maxiter`. Could also set `maxiter` large. To be safe, options with `maxfev` and `maxiter`. Recent scipy accepts `maxfev` for Powell. If old, maybe? Use `maxiter`? Powell documentation uses `maxfev` and `maxiter`. We'll include both. If initial_points multiple, each may use max_evals; total 3*300=900; okay. But set per attempt `maxfev= min(max_evals, max(100,...))`. We want maybe total 600. Use 200 each.

Potential issue: Powell calls objective many times and each direct evaluation; if direct evaluator very slow, maxfev important. Good.

`add_candidate`:

def add_candidate(pool, a, mode, evalscore, label):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    proxy=proxy_score(a, mode)
    if evalscore is None or not np.isfinite(evalscore): ev=np.inf
    else: ev=float(evalscore)
    pool.append((a, mode, ev, proxy, label))
    if len(pool) > 24:
       pool.sort(key=pool_sort_key)
       del pool[24:]

Optimization mode loop:
```
# first set deadline phases
phase_boundaries = []
if len(modes)==1:
    boundaries=[(modes[0], 0.45)]
elif len(modes)==2:
    boundaries=[(modes[0],0.40),(modes[1],0.20)]
# plus common polish 0.15, param 0.1, final 0.05 of total.
# Use absolute: (deadline-t0)
base_len_total = total - 30

def select_best(pool):
    best=None; best_val=np.inf; best_a=None
    for a,mode,ev,pr,label in pool:
        if ev is None or not np.isfinite(ev):
            ev=call_evaluator(a.tolist())
        if np.isfinite(ev):
            score=ev
        else:
            score=pr
        # prefer lower score; if tie prefer length same? no.
        if score < best_val - 1e-15:
            best_val=score; best_a=a.copy()
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=np.ones(6962)/6962; best_val=call_evaluator(...)
    return best_a,best_val

Potential bugs in `make_initial_candidates`: The list length and labels. Let's write:
```
def make_initial_candidates(n, rng, parent):
    cands=[]
    if parent is not None:
       p=resample_sequence(parent,n)
       p=normalize_nonneg(p)
       cands.append((p,'parent'))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cands.append((np.ones(n),'uniform'))
    cands.append((0.5-0.5*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x),'hann'))
    cands.append((0.54-0.46*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x),'hamming'))
    cands.append((0.42-0.5*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x)+0.08*np.cos(4.0*np.pi*x),'blackman'))
    bh=0.35875 - 0.48829*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x) + 0.14128*np.cos(4.0*np.pi*x) - 0.01168*np.cos(6.0*np.pi*x)
    cands.append((bh,'bh'))
    for w in (3.0,5.0,8.0,12.0):
       cands.append((np.exp(-w*(x-0.5)**2),'gauss'+str(int(w))))
    cands.append((np.maximum(0.0, 1.0-np.abs(2.0*x-1.0)), 'tri'))
    cands.append((0.5+0.5*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x),'edge_cos'))
    cands.append((0.5+0.25*np.cos(4.0*np.pi*x),'mid_cos'))
    # random smooth
    for r in range(6):
       v=smooth_noise(n, rng, 3.0 + 0.5*r)
       v=np.abs(v) + 0.1*np.exp(-4*(x-0.5)**2) + 1e-3
       cands.append((v,'rand_smooth%d'%r))
    # random positive
    v=np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))+1e-3
    cands.append((v,'rand_abs'))
    # heavy/light smooth
    v=np.maximum(1.0 - np.abs(2*x-1)**2, 1e-3); cands.append((v,'bell1'))
    v=np.maximum((1.0 - (2*x-1)**4),1e-3); cands.append((v,'bell4'))
    # sparse
    v=np.zeros(n); v[n//2]=1.0; cands.append((v,'onehot'))
    size=min(32,n)
    v=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,size=size,replace=False); v[idx]=1.0; cands.append((v,'sparse%d'%size))
    size=min(192,n)
    v=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,size=size,replace=False); v[idx]=1.0; cands.append((v,'sparse%d'%size))
    # clean
    out=[]
    for a,label in cands:
       a=normalize_nonneg(a)
       if a.size==n and np.isfinite(a).all(): out.append((a,label))
    if not out:
       out.append((np.ones(n)/float(n),'fallback'))
    return out
```
`smooth_noise` uses rng; for random smooth it generates zero-mean; adding abs makes nonnegative.

`smooth_noise` if n small, `m=n//2+1`. Need `amp=1.0/(1.0 + 0.2*np.arange(m))`, `spec[0]=0`.

`normalize_nonneg` for dense vectors. Good.

def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=max(120.0, float(budget_s))

    modes=select_search_modes(probe_mode, parent_eval)
    print('modes', modes)
    # n

       n=int(len(parent))
    else:
       n=6962
    rng=np.random.default_rng(777)

    # add explicit constant? make includes uniform 'uniform' but normalize; add 'constant' separately to be safe

    infos=[]

       pr0=proxy_score(a,0); pr1=proxy_score(a,1); infos.append((a,pr0,pr1,label))
    pool=[]
    # constant
    const_info = next? Need no lambda. Loop find label constant.

       if label == 'constant':
          ev=call_evaluator(a.tolist())
          add_candidate(pool,a,0,ev,label)
          break
    # parent

       a=normalize_nonng(resample_sequence(parent,n))
       pmode = 1 if probe_mode==1 or np.isfinite(parent_eval) < 1.995 else 0? Use mode 1? final no matter. Store parent_mode = modes[0] if modes else 0.
       add_candidate(pool,a, pmode, parent_eval, 'parent')
    # evaluate top off
    sorted_off=sorted(infos,key=info_key_off)
    evaluated_off=0
    for a,pr0,pr1,label in sorted_off:
       if evaluated_off>=8: break

          continue
       # avoid evaluating onehot? It's cheap but direct large; we want evaluate one-hot to see hidden metric. Include.
       ev=call_evaluator(a.tolist())
       add_candidate(pool,a,1,ev,label)
       evaluated_off+=1
    # if full in modes, evaluate top full maybe 4 (excluding already evaluated? easy)
    if 0 in modes:
       sorted_full=sorted(infos,key=info_key_full)
       evaluated_full=0
       # labels in pool? We can just evaluate top 4, duplicates okay? Avoid if label already in pool to save.
       existing= set()
       for item in pool: existing.add(item[4])
       for a,pr0,pr1,label in sorted_full:
          if evaluated_full>=4: break
          if label in existing: continue
          ev=call_evaluator(a.tolist())
          add_candidate(pool,a,0,ev,label)
          evaluated_full+=1
    # choose initial for each mode
    skip_opt = {'constant','onehot'} # maybe
    mode_inits={}
    for m in modes:
       sort_func = info_key_full if m==0 else info_key_off
       s=sorted(infos,key=sort_func)
       sel=[]

       for item in s:
          if item[3]=='parent': sel.append(item)
       if len(sel)<2:
          for item in s:
             if item[3] in skip_opt: continue
             if item[3]=='parent': continue
             if item not in sel: sel.append(item)
             if len(sel)>=2: break
       mode_inits[m]=sel
    # time fractions
    avail=soft_deadline-t0
    cur=0.0
    mode_deadlines=[]
    if len(modes)==1:
       fractions=[0.32]
    else:
       fractions=[0.25,0.13]
    for i,m in enumerate(modes):
       cur += fractions[i] if i<len(fractions) else 0.1
       md = t0 + avail*cur
       mode_deadlines.append((m, md))
    polish_frac=0.24 if len(modes)==1 else 0.20
    hill_frac=0.08 if len(modes)==1 else 0.04
    param_frac=0.16 if len(modes)==1 else 0.10
    cur += polish_frac; polish_deadline=t0+avail*cur
    cur += hill_frac; hill_deadline=t0+avail*cur
    cur += param_frac; param_deadline=t0+avail*cur
    # mode optimization
    for m, md in mode_deadlines:
       if time.monotonic() > md - 60: continue

          if time.monotonic() > md - 60: break
          a_lambda? no lambda. item is tuple (a,pr0,pr1,label)

          add_candidate(pool, best_a, m, best_ev, item[3]+':lbfgs')

    current=normalize_nonneg(a)
    best_a=current.copy()

       new=run_lbfgs(current,...)

       if pr < work_proxy - 1e-12:
           work_proxy=pr; work_a=new.copy()

       if pr <= work_proxy? work updated. new is work if improved. If no improvement, keep current (not new) to avoid drift.

    # high beta from best_a if eval finite else work_a

Now, let's verify LSE gradient formula with p array. For mode off, p[1:] weights. `p[::-1]` length n: p_rev[0]=p[n-1] etc. `fwd=irfft(A*Pr)[n-1:2n-1]`. Let's test with n=3, p1 weight only. Earlier formula: back=conv(a,p): p=[0,w,0]; conv(a,p) coeff [0, a0 w, a1 w? Wait a=[a0,a1,a2], p=[0,w,0]; conv = [0, a0 w, a1 w, a2 w]? length 5? polynomial a0 +a1z+a2z^2 * wz = a0w z + a1w z^2 + a2w z^3. slice back[0:3]=[0, a0w, a1w]. Derivative wrt a0 = a1, a1=a0+a2, a2=a1. sum back+fwd: j0 a1, j1 a0+a2, j2 a1. correct. Good.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing the provided
evaluate_sequence score.

The program uses several complementary strategies:
  1. A small probe to infer whether the hidden evaluator behaves like the
     full-lag score or another variant.
  2. A large pool of robust initial shapes: parent (if available), windows,
     Gaussians, smooth random shapes, sparse spikes, and constant sequence.
  3. Full projected/L-BFGS refinement of smooth log-sum-exp relaxations of
     peak convolution constraints.
  4. LP-alternating directions solved by FISTA, followed by peak-shaving and
     stochastic hill climbing.
  5. A low-dimensional black-box Powell search around the best candidate that
     calls evaluate_sequence directly, so it can adapt to the exact evaluator.

The final answer is the best sequence found according to evaluate_sequence,
with a constant fallback (which is optimal for the literal full-lag formula).
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def fft_len_for(full_len):
    if full_len <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (max(1, full_len - 1).bit_length())


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())
    return a / s


def get_parent_sequence():
    g = globals()
    if "height_sequence_1" not in g:
        return None
    p = g["height_sequence_1"]
    try:
        a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def self_conv_unique(a):
    """Return c[0]..c[n-1] for full linear self-convolution of a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    m_fft = fft_len_for(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, m_fft)[:n]


def proxy_score(a, mode):
    """
    Proxy score used for gradient-based search.
    mode 0: include lag 0 (full unique lags)
    mode 1: exclude lag 0
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = self_conv_unique(a)
    vals = c if mode == 0 else c[1:]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def call_evaluator(seq):
    try:
        v = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except NameError:
        return np.inf
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        v = float(v)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(v):
        return np.inf
    return v


def choose_probe_mode():
    """Infer which proxy is closer to the actual evaluator on small tests."""
    n = 128
    rng = np.random.default_rng(11)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    seqs = [
        np.ones(n),
        0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.exp(-5.0 * (x - 0.5) ** 2) + 0.1,
        np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 1.0,
    ]
    full_err = 0.0
    off_err = 0.0
    cnt = 0
    for v in seqs:
        a = normalize_nonneg(v)
        ev = call_evaluator(a.tolist())
        if not np.isfinite(ev):
            continue
        pf = proxy_score(a, 0)
        po = proxy_score(a, 1)
        scale = max(1.0, abs(ev))
        full_err += abs(ev - pf) / scale
        off_err += abs(ev - po) / scale
        cnt += 1
    if cnt == 0:
        return 1
    return 0 if full_err <= off_err else 1


def select_search_modes(probe_mode, parent_eval):
    """
    Always include the off-lag-0 direction because it is the main candidate
    for beating the literal full-lag lower bound. Include full-lag refinement
    only when the parent suggests its evaluator can go below the full bound.
    """
    modes = []
    if parent_eval is not None and np.isfinite(parent_eval) and parent_eval < 1.995:
        if probe_mode == 0:
            modes.append(0)
            modes.append(1)
        else:
            modes.append(1)
            modes.append(0)
    else:
        modes.append(1)
    out = []
    for m in modes:
        if m not in out:
            out.append(m)
    return out


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(xn, xo, a)


def normalize_basis(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    nv = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
    if not np.isfinite(nv) or nv < 1e-12:
        return np.zeros_like(v, dtype=np.float64)
    return v / nv


def smooth_noise(n, rng, decay=3.0):
    n_fft = fft_len_for(n + 1)
    m = n_fft // 2 + 1
    f = np.fft.rfftfreq(n_fft) * n_fft
    amp = 1.0 / (1.0 + 0.25 * np.maximum(0.0, f))
    re = rng.standard_normal(m) * amp
    im = rng.standard_normal(m) * amp
    spec = re + 1j * im
    spec[0] = 0.0
    v = np.fft.irfft(spec, n_fft).real[:n]
    return normalize_basis(v)


def make_initial_candidates(n, rng, parent):
    cands = []

    if parent is not None:
        p = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        cands.append((normalize_nonneg(p), "parent"))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    cands.append((np.ones(n), "uniform"))
    cands.append((0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x), "hann"))
    cands.append((0.54 - 0.46 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x), "hamming"))
    cands.append((0.42 - 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) +
                  0.08 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x), "blackman"))
    cands.append((0.35875 - 0.48829 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) +
                  0.14128 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x) -
                  0.01168 * np.cos(6.0 * np.pi * x), "bh"))

    for w in (3.0, 5.0, 8.0, 12.0):
        cands.append((np.exp(-w * (x - 0.5) ** 2), "gauss%g" % w))

    cands.append((np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)), "tri"))
    cands.append((0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x), "edge_cos"))
    cands.append((0.5 + 0.25 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x), "mid_cos"))
    cands.append((np.maximum(1e-6, 1.0 - (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2), "bell2"))
    cands.append((np.maximum(1e-6, 1.0 - (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 4), "bell4"))

    d0 = np.exp(-4.0 * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
    for r in range(6):
        v = smooth_noise(n, rng, 3.0 + 0.7 * r)
        v = np.abs(v) + 0.15 * d0 + 1e-3
        cands.append((v, "rand_smooth%d" % r))

    v = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 1e-3
    cands.append((v, "rand_abs"))

    # Sparse candidates can be decisive for many surrogate metrics.
    v = np.zeros(n)
    v[n // 2] = 1.0
    cands.append((v, "onehot"))

    for sz in (16, 64):
        if n <= 0:
            break
        v = np.zeros(n)
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=min(sz, n), replace=False)
        v[idx] = 1.0
        cands.append((v, "sparse%d" % int(sz)))

    out = []
    for a, lab in cands:
        a = normalize_nonneg(a)
        if a.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            out.append((a, lab))
    if not out:
        out.append((np.ones(n) / float(n), "fallback"))
    return out


def grad_selected(a, p):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k p_k c_k where c_k are unique lags 0..n-1 of a*a.
    p is length n and has zero entries for unselected lags.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    m_fft = fft_len_for(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, m_fft)
    Pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], m_fft)
    back = np.fft.irfft(A * P, m_fft)[:n]
    fwd = np.fft.irfft(A * Pr, m_fft)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return back + fwd


def lbfgs_objective(x, beta, mode, anchor):
    """
    Smoothed normalized peak objective and gradient.
    x is the unnormalized parameter vector; internally a = x / sum(x).
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    S = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = x / S
    m = 2 * n - 1
    m_fft = fft_len_for(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m_fft)[:n]

    if mode == 0:
        vals = c
        start = 0
    else:
        vals = c[1:]
        start = 1

    if vals.size == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    z = beta * (vals - cmax)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    ss = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
        p_sel = np.ones_like(vals, dtype=np.float64) / float(vals.size)
    else:
        p_sel = ez / ss

    L = cmax + (zmax + float(np.log(ss))) / beta
    value = float(2.0 * n * L) + float(anchor) * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)

    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[start:] = p_sel

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, m_fft)
    Pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], m_fft)
    back = np.fft.irfft(A * P, m_fft)[:n]
    fwd = np.fft.irfft(A * Pr, m_fft)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    grad_L = back + fwd

    grad_b = 2.0 * n * grad_L
    dot = float(np.dot(grad_b, a))
    grad_x = (grad_b - dot) / S
    grad_x = grad_x + 2.0 * anchor * (S - 1.0)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
        grad_x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return value, grad_x


def run_lbfgs(a, beta, mode, maxiter, maxfun, anchor=1e-9):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    x0 = a.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    opts = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 40,
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lbfgs_objective,
            x0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(beta, mode, anchor),
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception as e:
        print("  L-BFGS failed:", e)
        x = a.copy()

    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-9:
        return a
    return x / s


def run_lbfgs_schedule(a, mode, deadline, label):
    current = normalize_nonneg(a)
    best_a = current.copy()
    best_eval = call_evaluator(current.tolist())
    best_proxy = proxy_score(current, mode)

    work_a = current.copy()
    work_proxy = best_proxy

    schedule = [
        (10.0, 300, 1200),
        (30.0, 400, 1800),
        (100.0, 600, 2500),
        (300.0, 800, 3000),
        (1000.0, 1200, 4000),
    ]

    for beta, mi, mf in schedule:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 25.0:
            break
        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - now)
        if remaining < 25.0:
            break
        if remaining < 80.0:
            mf = max(200, int(mf // 4))
        elif remaining < 150.0:
            mf = max(400, int(mf // 2))

        print("    [%s] L-BFGS beta=%.0f maxfun=%d remaining=%.0fs"
              % (label, beta, mf, remaining), flush=True)

        work_before = work_proxy
        best_eval_before = best_eval
        best_proxy_before = best_proxy

        new = run_lbfgs(current, beta, mode, mi, mf, 1e-9)
        new = normalize_nonneg(new)
        pr = proxy_score(new, mode)
        ev = call_evaluator(new.tolist())

        improved_eval = np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_eval_before - 1e-12
        improved_proxy = pr < best_proxy_before - 1e-12

        if improved_eval:
            best_eval = ev
            best_a = new.copy()
        if improved_proxy:
            best_proxy = pr
            if not np.isfinite(best_eval_before):
                best_a = new.copy()

        advanced = False
        if pr < work_before - 1e-12:
            work_proxy = pr
            work_a = new.copy()
            current = new.copy()
            advanced = True

        if not advanced and improved_eval:
            work_proxy = pr
            work_a = new.copy()
            current = new.copy()
            advanced = True

        print("    [%s] beta=%.0f proxy=%.8f eval=%.8f"
              % (label, beta, pr, ev), flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        start = best_a if np.isfinite(best_eval) else work_a
        print("    [%s] high-beta polish" % label, flush=True)
        new = run_lbfgs(start, 5000.0, mode, 1500, 6000, 1e-10)
        new = normalize_nonneg(new)
        pr = proxy_score(new, mode)
        ev = call_evaluator(new.tolist())
        if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_eval - 1e-12:
            best_eval = ev
            best_a = new.copy()
        if pr < work_proxy - 1e-12:
            work_proxy = pr
            work_a = new.copy()

    return (best_a if np.isfinite(best_eval) else work_a), best_eval, work_a


def lp_fista(a, mode, M, max_iters, beta, x_init, deadline, linear_coeff=1.0):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if x_init is not None:
        x = np.asarray(x_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size != n:
            x = resample_sequence(x, n)
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        if float(x.sum()) < 1e-12:
            x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    u = x.copy()
    tv = 1.0
    m = 2 * n - 1
    m_fft = fft_len_for(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
    sum_a2 = float(np.dot(a, a))
    L = beta * (4.0 * float(n) * sum_a2 + 1.0) + 1e-12
    step = 1.0 / max(1e-30, L)
    start = 0 if mode == 0 else 1

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        B = np.fft.rfft(x, m_fft)
        cfull = np.fft.irfft(A * B, m_fft)[:m]
        vals = cfull[start:]
        if vals.size == 0:
            break

        z = beta * (vals - M)
        zmax = float(np.max(z))
        ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
        ss = float(ez.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            p_sel = np.ones_like(vals, dtype=np.float64) / float(vals.size)
        else:
            p_sel = ez / ss

        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p[start:] = p_sel
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, m_fft)
        Bt = np.fft.irfft(A * P, m_fft)[:n]
        grad = Bt - float(linear_coeff)

        v = u - step * grad
        x_new = np.maximum(v, 0.0)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
            x_new = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        tnew = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * tv * tv))
        u = x_new + ((tv - 1.0) / tnew) * (x_new - x)
        u = np.maximum(u, 0.0)
        x = x_new
        tv = tnew

    return x


def lp_alternating(a, mode, deadline, max_outers=4, fista_iters=3000, beta=400.0):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_pr = proxy_score(a, mode)
    x_warm = None
    stall = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_outers)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        c = self_conv_unique(a)
        vals = c if mode == 0 else c[1:]
        if vals.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(np.max(vals))
        x = lp_fista(a, mode, M, fista_iters, beta, x_warm, deadline)
        x_warm = x.copy()
        sx = float(x.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sx) or sx < 1e-12:
            break
        xn = x / sx

        best_t = 0.0
        best_t_pr = best_pr
        for t in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 1.00):
            at = normalize_nonneg((1.0 - t) * a + t * xn)
            pr = proxy_score(at, mode)
            if pr < best_t_pr - 1e-13:
                best_t_pr = pr
                best_t = t

        if best_t <= 0.0:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 2:
                break
            continue

        at = normalize_nonng((1.0 - best_t) * a + best_t * xn)
        pr = best_t_pr
        if pr < best_pr - 1e-13:
            best = at.copy()
            best_pr = pr
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
        a = at
        if stall >= 4:
            break

    return best


def peak_search(a, mode, deadline, rng=None, max_steps=500):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(2)
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_pr = proxy_score(a, mode)
    n = a.size
    stall = 0

    for stp in range(int(max_steps)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        c = self_conv_unique(a)
        vals = c if mode == 0 else c[1:]
        if vals.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(np.max(vals))
        mlen = vals.size
        ktop = max(1, int(round(max(3.0, 0.001 * mlen))))
        if ktop >= mlen:
            thresh = 0.0
        else:
            thresh = float(np.partition(vals, -ktop)[-ktop])

        tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, M)
        active = np.where(vals >= thresh - tol)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.where(vals == M)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(vals))], dtype=int)

        p_sel = np.ones(active.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(active.size)
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if mode == 0:
            p[active] = p_sel
        else:
            p[active + 1] = p_sel

        g = grad_selected(a, p)
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0

        improved = False
        if np.isfinite(maxg) and maxg > 1e-18:
            base = min(10.0, (float(a.sum()) / float(n)) * 0.05 / maxg)
            for mult in (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0):
                d = base * mult
                at = normalize_nonneg(a - d * g)
                pr = proxy_score(at, mode)
                if pr < best_pr - 1e-13:
                    best_pr = pr
                    best = at.copy()
                    a = at.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break

        if not improved:
            if stp % 5 == 0:
                noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, 3.0)
                at = normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(a * (1.0 + 0.02 * noise), 0.0))
                pr = proxy_score(at, mode)
                if pr < best_pr - 1e-13:
                    best_pr = pr
                    best = at.copy()
                    a = at.copy()
            else:
                stall += 1

        stall = 0 if improved else stall + 1
        if stall > 50:
            break

    return best


def random_hill(a, rng, mode, deadline, max_tries=300):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_pr = proxy_score(a, mode)
    best_ev = call_evaluator(best.tolist())
    n = a.size
    stall = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_tries)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        improved = False

        # Additive smooth directions.
        d = smooth_noise(n, rng, 3.0)
        for mult in (0.005, 0.01, 0.03, 0.08):
            for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                at = normalize_nonneg(a + sign * mult * d)
                pr = proxy_score(at, mode)
                if pr < best_pr - 1e-11:
                    ev = call_evaluator(at.tolist())
                    if (not np.isfinite(best_ev)) and (not np.isfinite(ev)):
                        ok = pr < best_pr
                    elif np.isfinite(ev) and np.isfinite(best_ev):
                        ok = ev < best_ev - 1e-12
                    else:
                        ok = False
                    if ok:
                        best = at.copy()
                        best_pr = pr
                        best_ev = ev if np.isfinite(ev) else np.inf
                        a = at.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break
            if improved:
                break

        if not improved:
            noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, 3.0)
            for s in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20):
                at = normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(a * (1.0 + s * noise), 0.0))
                pr = proxy_score(at, mode)
                if pr < best_pr - 1e-11:
                    ev = call_evaluator(at.tolist())
                    if (not np.isfinite(best_ev)) and (not np.isfinite(ev)):
                        ok = pr < best_pr
                    elif np.isfinite(ev) and np.isfinite(best_ev):
                        ok = ev < best_ev - 1e-12
                    else:
                        ok = False
                    if ok:
                        best = at.copy()
                        best_pr = pr
                        best_ev = ev if np.isfinite(ev) else np.inf
                        a = at.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break

        stall = 0 if improved else stall + 1
        if stall > 60:
            break

    return best, best_ev


def build_bases(n, rng, count):
    if count <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.zeros((max(0, n), 0), dtype=np.float64)

    bases = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    for f in range(1, min(10, max(3, count // 2 + 1))):
        if len(bases) < count:
            bases.append(normalize_basis(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x)))
        if len(bases) < count:
            bases.append(normalize_basis(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x)))

    m = n // 2 + 1
    while len(bases) < count:
        amp = 1.0 / (1.0 + 0.2 * np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64))
        re = rng.standard_normal(m) * amp
        im = rng.standard_normal(m) * amp
        spec = re + 1j * im
        spec[0] = 0.0
        v = np.fft.irfft(spec, n).real
        bases.append(normalize_basis(v))

    bases = bases[:count]
    return np.stack(bases, axis=1)


def param_objective(coeffs, base, bases, scale):
    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if coeffs.size == 0:
        return call_evaluator(base.tolist())
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(coeffs)):
        return np.inf
    a = base.copy()
    for i in range(coeffs.size):
        c = float(coeffs[i])
        if c != 0.0:
            a += scale * c * bases[:, i]
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    return call_evaluator(a.tolist())


def param_search(base, deadline, max_evals=300, K=18, scale=0.25):
    n = base.size
    rng = np.random.default_rng(98765 + max(1, n))
    bases = build_bases(n, rng, max(1, int(K)))
    K = bases.shape[1]

    best_val = call_evaluator(base.tolist())
    best_coeffs = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)

    starts = [
        np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64),
        rng.standard_normal(K) * 0.05,
        rng.standard_normal(K) * 0.15,
    ]

    opts = {
        "maxfev": int(max_evals),
        "maxiter": 120,
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "xtol": 1e-8,
        "disp": False,
    }

    for x0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                param_objective,
                x0,
                method="Powell",
                args=(base, bases, scale),
                options=opts,
            )
            xopt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            val = param_objective(xopt, base, bases, scale)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val - 1e-12:
                best_val = val
                best_coeffs = xopt.copy()
        except Exception as e:
            print("    param search failed:", e)

    out = base.copy()
    for i in range(best_coeffs.size):
        c = float(best_coeffs[i])
        if c != 0.0:
            out += scale * c * bases[:, i]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        out = base
    else:
        out = out / s
    return out, best_val


def info_key_full(info):
    return info[1]


def info_key_off(info):
    return info[2]


def pool_sort_key(item):
    ev = item[2]
    if np.isfinite(ev):
        return (0, float(ev))
    return (1, float(item[3]))


def add_candidate(pool, a, mode, evalscore, label):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    pr = proxy_score(a, mode)
    if evalscore is None or not np.isfinite(evalscore):
        ev = np.inf
    else:
        ev = float(evalscore)
    pool.append((a, mode, ev, pr, label))
    if len(pool) > 30:
        pool.sort(key=pool_sort_key)
        del pool[30:]


def select_best(pool, deadline):
    ordered = sorted(pool, key=pool_sort_key)
    best_val = np.inf
    best_a = None
    best_label = ""
    for a, mode, ev, pr, lab in ordered:
        if not np.isfinite(ev):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 4.0:
                break
            ev = call_evaluator(a.tolist())
        if np.isfinite(ev):
            score = ev
        else:
            score = pr
        if score < best_val - 1e-15:
            best_val = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_label = lab
    if best_a is None:
        n = 6962
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_val = call_evaluator(best_a.tolist())
        best_label = "fallback_constant"
    return best_a, best_val, best_label


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    soft_deadline = t0 + total - 25.0

    print("Starting propose_candidate: budget=%.1fs" % total, flush=True)

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    parent_eval = np.inf
    if parent is not None:
        parent_eval = call_evaluator(parent.tolist())
        print("Parent length=%d parent_eval=%.8f" % (parent.size, parent_eval), flush=True)
    else:
        print("No parent available.", flush=True)

    probe_mode = choose_probe_mode()
    modes = select_search_modes(probe_mode, parent_eval)
    print("Probe mode=%d search_modes=%s" % (probe_mode, modes), flush=True)

    if parent is not None and 2048 <= parent.size <= 12000:
        n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n = 6962
    print("Working length n=%d" % n, flush=True)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240517)
    cands = make_initial_candidates(n, rng, parent)

    const = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    cands.append((normalize_nonneg(const), "constant"))

    infos = []
    for a, lab in cands:
        pr0 = proxy_score(a, 0)
        pr1 = proxy_score(a, 1)
        infos.append((a, pr0, pr1, lab))

    pool = []

    # Constant is always in the pool.
    for a, pr0, pr1, lab in infos:
        if lab == "constant":
            ev = call_evaluator(a.tolist())
            add_candidate(pool, a, 0, ev, "constant")
            print("Constant eval=%.8f" % ev, flush=True)
            break

    # Parent, if available, is always in the pool.
    if parent is not None and np.isfinite(parent_eval):
        a_parent = normalize_nonng(resample_sequence(parent, n))
        parent_mode = modes[0] if modes else 0
        add_candidate(pool, a_parent, parent_mode, parent_eval, "parent")

    # Evaluate a reasonable set of top candidates using the real evaluator.
    evaluated_labels = set(item[4] for item in pool)

    sorted_off = sorted(infos, key=info_key_off)
    cnt = 0
    for a, pr0, pr1, lab in sorted_off:
        if cnt >= 8:
            break
        if lab in evaluated_labels:
            continue
        ev = call_evaluator(a.tolist())
        add_candidate(pool, a, 1, ev, lab)
        evaluated_labels.add(lab)
        cnt += 1

    if 0 in modes:
        sorted_full = sorted(infos, key=info_key_full)
        cnt = 0
        for a, pr0, pr1, lab in sorted_full:
            if cnt >= 5:
                break
            if lab in evaluated_labels:
                continue
            ev = call_evaluator(a.tolist())
            add_candidate(pool, a, 0, ev, lab)
            evaluated_labels.add(lab)
            cnt += 1

    # Choose a couple of starting points for each refined mode.
    skip_opt = {"constant", "onehot"}
    mode_inits = {}
    for m in modes:
        s = sorted(infos, key=(info_key_full if m == 0 else info_key_off))
        sel = []
        sel_labels = []

        for item in s:
            if item[3] == "parent" and "parent" not in sel_labels:
                sel.append(item)
                sel_labels.append(item[3])

        for item in s:
            lab = item[3]
            if lab in skip_opt:
                continue
            if lab in sel_labels:
                continue
            sel.append(item)
            sel_labels.append(lab)
            if len(sel) >= 2:
                break
        mode_inits[m] = sel

    avail = max(1.0, soft_deadline - t0)

    # Time allocation.
    if len(modes) == 1:
        mode_fracs = [0.30]
        polish_frac = 0.26
        hill_frac = 0.07
        param_frac = 0.17
    else:
        mode_fracs = [0.24, 0.12]
        polish_frac = 0.22
        hill_frac = 0.04
        param_frac = 0.11

    frac = 0.0
    mode_deadlines = []
    for i, m in enumerate(modes):
        if i < len(mode_fracs):
            frac += mode_fracs[i]
        else:
            frac += 0.08
        mode_deadlines.append((m, t0 + avail * frac))

    frac += polish_frac
    polish_deadline = t0 + avail * frac
    frac += hill_frac
    hill_deadline = t0 + avail * frac
    frac += param_frac
    param_deadline = min(soft_deadline, t0 + avail * frac)

    # Gradient/L-BFGS refinement on selected modes.
    for m, md in mode_deadlines:
        if time.monotonic() > md - 45.0:
            continue
        inits = mode_inits.get(m, [])
        for item in inits:
            if time.monotonic() > md - 45.0:
                break
            init_a = item[0]
            lab = item[3]
            best_a, best_ev, work_a = run_lbfgs_schedule(init_a, m, md, lab)
            add_candidate(pool, best_a, m, best_ev, lab + ":lbfgs")
            if work_a is not None:
                add_candidate(pool, work_a, m, None, lab + ":work")

    # Polish the best robust candidates.
    pool.sort(key=pool_sort_key)
    polish_items = []
    for item in pool:
        lab = item[4]
        if lab in skip_opt or "onehot" in lab:
            continue
        polish_items.append(item)
        if len(polish_items) >= 2:
            break

    if polish_items and time.monotonic() < polish_deadline - 20.0:
        n_polish = len(polish_items)
        for idx, item in enumerate(polish_items):
            cur = time.monotonic()
            remaining = max(0.0, polish_deadline - cur)
            sub = remaining / max(1, n_polish - idx)
            if sub < 20.0:
                break
            a, mode, ev, pr, lab = item
            lp_deadline = cur + 0.55 * sub
            peak_deadline = cur + sub
            try:
                a_lp = lp_alternating(a, mode, lp_deadline,
                                      max_outers=3, fista_iters=2500, beta=500.0)
                ev_lp = call_evaluator(a_lp.tolist())
                add_candidate(pool, a_lp, mode, ev_lp, lab + ":lp")
            except Exception as e:
                print("    LP polish failed:", e)
            if time.monotonic() < peak_deadline - 10.0:
                a_pk = peak_search(a, mode, peak_deadline, rng, max_steps=250)
                ev_pk = call_evaluator(a_pk.tolist())
                add_candidate(pool, a_pk, mode, ev_pk, lab + ":peak")

    # Black-box stochastic hill climb on the current best.
    pool.sort(key=pool_sort_key)
    if pool and time.monotonic() < hill_deadline - 15.0:
        top = pool[0]
        a_hill, ev_hill = random_hill(top[0], rng, top[1], hill_deadline, max_tries=250)
        add_candidate(pool, a_hill, top[1], ev_hill, "hill")

    # Low-dimensional direct black-box search around the best candidate(s).
    pool.sort(key=pool_sort_key)
    param_tries = 0
    for item in pool:
        if time.monotonic() > param_deadline - 60.0:
            break
        if param_tries >= 1:
            break
        lab = item[4]
        if lab in skip_opt or "onehot" in lab:
            continue
        a_p, ev_p = param_search(item[0], param_deadline,
                                 max_evals=260, K=18, scale=0.25)
        add_candidate(pool, a_p, item[1], ev_p, "param_" + lab)
        param_tries += 1

    # Final selection.
    pool.sort(key=pool_sort_key)
    best_a, best_score, best_label = select_best(pool, soft_deadline)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    if not out or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    final_score = call_evaluator(out)
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print("=" * 70, flush=True)
    print("FINAL best_label=%s length=%d" % (best_label, len(out)), flush=True)
    print("INTERNAL best_score=%.12f" % best_score, flush=True)
    print("VERIFIED final_score=%.12f" % final_score, flush=True)
    print("Elapsed=%.1fs / %.1fs" % (elapsed, total), flush=True)
    print("=" * 70, flush=True)

    return out
```
